Policy Tracker

Steps and code for the Policy Tracker app. The code on this page is your live app as saved in the Screen YAML Vault on 25/11/2026, plus the changes marked Changed.

Update 25/11/2026: stand-down periods added. You set a start and end date in a SharePoint list and nobody can plan a release on any Thursday in between (both dates included). It changes App.Formulas, App.OnStart and ViewItem (Steps 2 to 4). Nothing else in the app changes.
Update 25/11/2026: the Newsletter Title and Audience required rule is now in the ViewItem paste (Step 4). It had been prepared earlier but your live app did not have it yet.
Guide rebuilt 25/11/2026: the old Fix 1 to 10 list has been cleared out because all of it is live and running. From now on this page holds the complete current code for the app, with a date on every block.

What is in this build

Work down the page and tick each step off. Copy works without opening the code. Steps 1 to 4 are the ones to do. Steps 5 to 9 are your current screens, kept here so the page always has the whole app with dates. You only need to paste them if you are rebuilding a screen.

Before you start: open the app's Details page in make.powerapps.com, go to the Versions tab and note the latest version number, so you can restore it if anything misbehaves.

1The stand-down list

The list PolicyTracker-StandDownPeriods is already created. Check it has these columns, then in Studio choose Data → Add data → SharePoint, pick the list, and confirm the data source is called PolicyTracker-StandDownPeriods.

ColumnTypeNotes
TitleSingle line of textThe reason people see, for example "Christmas leave"
StartDateDate onlyFirst day of the stand-down
EndDateDate onlyLast day of the stand-down (included)
ActiveYes/NoDefault Yes. Set to No to switch a period off without deleting it
Check these two names. The code expects the reason column to be Title and the Yes/No column to be Active. If yours are called something else, tell me before you paste and I will change the code.

2App.Formulas

Select App → Formulas, replace everything, paste. Three new functions sit just above RefreshNewsletterPack: StandDownReason, FirstOpenThursday and LoadStandDown. Everything else is as it is in Studio today.

GetFileExt(LinkUrl: Text): Text = With(
    {FileParamStart: Find("file=", LinkUrl, 1)},
    With(
        {
            FileNamePart: If(
                FileParamStart > 0,
                With(
                    {AmpPos: Find("&", LinkUrl, FileParamStart + 5)},
                    Mid(LinkUrl, FileParamStart + 5, If(AmpPos > 0, AmpPos - (FileParamStart + 5), Len(LinkUrl) - (FileParamStart + 5) + 1))
                ),
                If(Find("?", LinkUrl, 1) > 0, Left(LinkUrl, Find("?", LinkUrl, 1) - 1), LinkUrl)
            )
        },
        Switch(
            true,
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".docx", "docx",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".docm", "docm",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".dotx", "dotx",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".dotm", "dotm",
            Right(FileNamePart, 4) = ".doc", "doc",
            Right(FileNamePart, 4) = ".dot", "dot",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".xlsx", "xlsx",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".xlsm", "xlsm",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".xltx", "xltx",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".xltm", "xltm",
            Right(FileNamePart, 4) = ".xls", "xls",
            Right(FileNamePart, 4) = ".xlt", "xlt",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".pptx", "pptx",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".pptm", "pptm",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".potx", "potx",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".potm", "potm",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".ppsx", "ppsx",
            Right(FileNamePart, 5) = ".ppsm", "ppsm",
            Right(FileNamePart, 4) = ".ppt", "ppt",
            Right(FileNamePart, 4) = ".pot", "pot",
            Right(FileNamePart, 4) = ".pps", "pps",
            Right(FileNamePart, 4) = ".pdf", "pdf",
            ""
        )
    )
);
BuildLinksForSource(SourceID: Number): Boolean = {
    ClearCollect(
        colLinks,
        AddColumns(
            Filter(PolicyLinks, PolicyTrackerSource.Id = SourceID),
            FileExt,
            GetFileExt(LinkUrl)
        )
    );
    Set(varLinksSourceID, SourceID);
    true
};
RefreshActiveCache(): Boolean = {
    ClearCollect(
        colActiveItems,
        Filter(
            'Policy Proof Tracker',
            'Planned Publish Date' >= Today() &&
            'Planned Publish Date' <= DateAdd(Today(), 28, "Days")
        )
    );
    ClearCollect(
        colActiveLinks,
        AddColumns(
            Filter(PolicyLinks, PolicyTrackerSource.Id in colActiveItems.ID),
            FileExt,
            GetFileExt(LinkUrl)
        )
    );
    Set(varActiveCacheReady, true);
    true
};
NextThursday(WeeksAhead: Number): Date = With(
    {_d: DateAdd(Today(), Mod(5 - Weekday(Today()) + 7, 7) + (WeeksAhead - 1) * 7, "Days")},
    Date(Year(_d), Month(_d), Day(_d))
);
StandDownReason(d: DateTime): Text = If(
    IsBlank(d),
    "",
    With(
        {_d: Date(Year(d), Month(d), Day(d))},
        If(
            _d < Today(),
            "",
            With(
                {_p: LookUp(colStandDown, _d >= StartD && _d <= EndD)},
                If(IsBlank(_p), "", Coalesce(Trim(_p.Title), "Stand down"))
            )
        )
    )
);
FirstOpenThursday(): Date = Coalesce(
    First(
        Filter(
            ForAll(Sequence(104), {d: NextThursday(Value)}),
            StandDownReason(d) = ""
        )
    ).d,
    NextThursday(1)
);
LoadStandDown(): Boolean = {
    IfError(
        ClearCollect(
            colStandDown,
            AddColumns(
                Filter(
                    Filter('PolicyTracker-StandDownPeriods', EndDate >= Today()),
                    Coalesce(Active, false)
                ),
                StartD, Date(Year(StartDate), Month(StartDate), Day(StartDate)),
                EndD, Date(Year(EndDate), Month(EndDate), Day(EndDate))
            )
        ),
        Notify("Could not read the PolicyTracker-StandDownPeriods list, so stand-down dates may not be enforced. Check your connection and reopen the item.", NotificationType.Warning)
    );
    Set(varFirstOpenThursday, FirstOpenThursday());
    true
};
RefreshNewsletterPack(): Boolean = {
    Set(varNewsletterReady, false);
    Set(varNewsletterTargetDate, NextThursday(1));
    Refresh('Policy Proof Tracker');
    ClearCollect(
        colNewsletterPack,
        Sort(
            AddColumns(
                Filter(
                    'Policy Proof Tracker',
                    'Planned Publish Date' >= varNewsletterTargetDate &&
                    'Planned Publish Date' < DateAdd(varNewsletterTargetDate, 1, "Days") &&
                    (
                        'Approved For Release By'.Value = "XO" ||
                        'Approved For Release By'.Value = "ACOS" ||
                        'Approved For Release By'.Value = "DCOMD" ||
                        'Approved For Release By'.Value = "D Commander"
                    ) &&
                    Published = false &&
                    Coalesce('External consultation required', false) = false
                ),
                SortKey,
                If(
                    'Top Item',
                    -1000,
                    Coalesce(LookUp(colChoiceColors, ChoiceValue = 'Document Type'.Value, SortOrder), 99)
                ),
                IsFinalApproved,
                Or(
                    'Approved For Release By'.Value = "XO",
                    'Approved For Release By'.Value = "ACOS",
                    'Approved For Release By'.Value = "DCOMD",
                    'Approved For Release By'.Value = "D Commander"
                ),
                PackTitle,
                Coalesce('Title for Newsletter', Title),
                PackAudience,
                Coalesce(Audience, ""),
                PackSummary,
                Coalesce('Item summary', "")
            ),
            SortKey,
            SortOrder.Ascending
        )
    );
    ClearCollect(colBatchGroups, AddColumns(Sort('BM Groups', SortOrder, SortOrder.Ascending), Ticked, false));
    ClearCollect(colBatchMemberships, Filter('BM Memberships', false));
    ForAll(colBatchGroups As grp, Collect(colBatchMemberships, Filter('BM Memberships', GroupName = grp.Title)));
    Set(varNewsletterSubject, Text(Today(), "yyyymmdd") & " - Cadets Branch weekly policy updates");
    Set(
        varNewsletterBodyStatic,
        "<br><b>Previous Newsletters:</b><br>" &
        "All the previous weekly policy updates are available via the links below<br>" &
        "MODNet users: <a href=""https://modgovuk.sharepoint.com/teams/1598/SitePages/Weekly-Policy-Updates---Archive.aspx"">Weekly Policy Updates (sharepoint.com)</a><br>" &
        "Resource Centre users: Navigate to: Westminster → Resource Centre → Organisation → Cadets Branch Weekly Policy Updates<br><br>" &
        "<b>About the Newsletter:</b><br>" &
        "This newsletter works on both MODNet and personal systems, and all policies included will also be uploaded on to both MODNet and the Resource Centre for routine accessing.<br>" &
        "Each week we will send out a version of this email, to the usual recipients, providing a link to the latest policy newsletter and a summary of what items have been included.<br>" &
        "This email has been sent to the groups listed below but please feel free to forward on as you deem necessary, emailed recipients:<br><br>" &
        "ACF CEOs (ex-HQ SE)<br>" &
        "JMC SO2's<br>" &
        "JMC OC CTT's<br>" &
        "---<br>" &
        "JMC Cadets Branch 0mailboxes<br>" &
        "JMC CTT 0mailboxes<br>" &
        "JMC Col Cadets (including Col Ashley Fulford)<br>" &
        "JMC RAL members<br>" &
        "JMC SCEOs<br>" &
        "Cadets Branch<br>" &
        "CTC Frimley<br>" &
        "CCAT<br>" &
        "CRFCA (Sam Plant and Simon Estick)<br>" &
        "ACCT UK (Murdo Urquhart and Richard Walton)<br>" &
        "CCRS (Shooting Manager - Daf Marston)<br>" &
        "CCF RAF &amp; RN leads"
    );
    Set(varNewsletterReady, true);
    true
};
CleanDocumentUrl(RawUrl: Text): Text = With(
    {_trimmed: Trim(RawUrl)},
    With(
        {
            _cleaned:
                Left(
                    _trimmed,
                    Min(
                        If(Find("&action", _trimmed) > 0, Find("&action", _trimmed) - 1, Len(_trimmed)),
                        If(Find("&clickparams", _trimmed) > 0, Find("&clickparams", _trimmed) - 1, Len(_trimmed)),
                        If(Find("&CID=", _trimmed) > 0, Find("&CID=", _trimmed) - 1, Len(_trimmed)),
                        If(Find("&wdLOR", _trimmed) > 0, Find("&wdLOR", _trimmed) - 1, Len(_trimmed))
                    )
                )
        },
        With(
            {_idPos: Find("id=%2F", _cleaned)},
            If(
                Find("/Forms/AllItems.aspx?", _cleaned) > 0 && _idPos > 0,
                With(
                    {_afterId: Mid(_cleaned, _idPos + 3, Len(_cleaned))},
                    Left(_cleaned, Find("/", _cleaned, 9) - 1) &
                    Substitute(
                        Substitute(
                            Substitute(
                                Substitute(
                                    Left(_afterId, If(Find("&", _afterId) > 0, Find("&", _afterId) - 1, Len(_afterId))),
                                    "%2F", "/"
                                ),
                                "%2E", "."
                            ),
                            "%2D", "-"
                        ),
                        "%5F", "_"
                    )
                ),
                _cleaned
            )
        )
    )
);

4ViewItem

In the Tree view delete the ViewItem screen, add a blank screen named exactly ViewItem, then paste. Other screens will show red errors for a moment where they navigate to ViewItem. They clear as soon as the new ViewItem is in. The paste is long (about 3,700 lines), so give it a moment.

What changed on this screen:
  • In Pick a Thursday, stand-down Thursdays are greyed out, tagged STAND DOWN with your reason, and cannot be chosen. Tapping one tells the person why.
  • If an item's date is already inside a stand-down, a red note appears under the date field, and Save, Save & Close and the Add Document Link buttons refuse until it is moved.
  • High Priority does not override a stand-down. It is a hard lock.
  • In the Duplicate dialog, stand-down Thursdays are simply left out of the list.
  • A new item no longer starts on a stand-down Thursday. It starts on the first Thursday that is open.
  • Newsletter Title and Audience are now required. Save, Save & Close and Add Document Link are greyed out until both are filled in, and an amber banner under the top bar says which one is missing. Items ticked External consultation required or Published are exempt.

Screens:
  ViewItem:
    Properties:
      Fill: =RGBA(244, 246, 243, 1)
      LoadingSpinnerColor: =RGBA(11, 74, 54, 1)
      OnVisible: |-
        =LoadStandDown();
        Set(varLoadingLinks, varLinksSourceID <> varSelectedRelease.ID || IsBlank(varSelectedRelease.ID));
        Set(varClosingViewItem, false);
        Set(varSaving, false);
        Set(varShowLinkModal, false);
        Set(varSelectedLink, Blank());
        Set(varErrTitle, false);
        Set(varErrOwner, false);
        Set(varErrDocType, false);
        Set(varErrPublishDate, false);
        Set(
            varOwnerDefault,
            If(
                IsBlank(varSelectedRelease) || IsBlank(varSelectedRelease.Owner.Email),
                Table({DisplayName: User().FullName, Mail: User().Email}),
                Table({DisplayName: varSelectedRelease.Owner.DisplayName, Mail: varSelectedRelease.Owner.Email})
            )
        );
        Set(
            varPlannedPublishDate,
            If(
                IsBlank(varSelectedRelease) || IsBlank(varSelectedRelease.'Planned Publish Date'),
                varFirstOpenThursday,
                varSelectedRelease.'Planned Publish Date'
            )
        );
        If(
            !varDeepLinkHandled && !IsBlank(Param("ID")),
            Set(varDeepLinkHandled, true);
            Set(varItemID, Param("ID"));
            Set(varLoading, true);
            Refresh('Policy Proof Tracker');
            Set(varDeepLinkItem, LookUp('Policy Proof Tracker', ID = Value(varItemID)));
            If(
                !IsBlank(varDeepLinkItem),
                Set(varSelectedRelease, varDeepLinkItem);
                Set(varSelectPolicy, "Filled");
                Set(varParentSaved, true);
                Set(varSelectedItem, varDeepLinkItem);
                Set(varSourceScreen, "Main");
                Set(varSelectedPerson, varDeepLinkItem.Owner);
                Refresh(PolicyLinks);
                BuildLinksForSource(varDeepLinkItem.ID);
                Set(varOwnerDefault, Table({DisplayName: varDeepLinkItem.Owner.DisplayName, Mail: varDeepLinkItem.Owner.Email}));
                Set(varPlannedPublishDate, varDeepLinkItem.'Planned Publish Date'),
                Notify("This item could not be found. It may have been deleted or the link may be incorrect.", NotificationType.Error);
                Set(varDeepLinkNotFound, true)
            );
            Set(varLoading, false)
        );
        Reset(txtTitle1);
        Reset(drpDocType1);
        Reset(dpkPublishDate1);
        Reset(cmb_Owner1);
        Reset(ckbHighPriority1);
        Reset(txtReasonHighPri1);

        Reset(txtExtConsultDetails1);
        Reset(ckbTopItem1);
        Reset(txtNewsletterTitle1);
        Reset(txtAudience1);
        Reset(txtSummary1);
        Reset(txtRemarks1);
        Reset(ckbPillarLead1);
        Reset(radApproval1);
        Reset(ckbPublished1);
        Reset(dpkPublishedDate1);
        Set(varShowThursdayPicker, false);
        ClearCollect(
            colThursdayItemCounts,
            AddColumns(
                GroupBy(
                    AddColumns(
                        Filter(
                            'Policy Proof Tracker',
                            'Planned Publish Date' >= Today() &&
                            'Planned Publish Date' <= DateAdd(Today(), 104 * 7, "Days")
                        ),
                        GroupDate, 'Planned Publish Date'
                    ),
                    GroupDate,
                    GroupedItems
                ),
                DayItemCount,
                CountRows(GroupedItems)
            )
        );
        ClearCollect(
            colThursdayOptions,
            ForAll(
                Sequence(104),
                With(
                    {_thu: NextThursday(Value)},
                    {
                        ThursdayDate: _thu,
                        ItemCount: Coalesce(LookUp(colThursdayItemCounts, GroupDate = _thu, DayItemCount), 0),
                        StandDownReason: StandDownReason(_thu)
                    }
                )
            )
        );
        If(
            !IsBlank(varSelectedRelease.ID),
            If(
                !IsBlank(LookUp(colActiveItems, ID = varSelectedRelease.ID)),
                ClearCollect(colLinks, Filter(colActiveLinks, PolicyTrackerSource.Id = varSelectedRelease.ID));
                Set(varLinksSourceID, varSelectedRelease.ID),
                If(
                    varLinksSourceID <> varSelectedRelease.ID,
                    BuildLinksForSource(varSelectedRelease.ID)
                )
            );
            Set(varLoadingLinks, false),
            Clear(colLinks);
            Set(varLoadingLinks, false)
        );

                              colUpcomingEntries,
                              AddColumns(
                                  Filter(
                                      'Policy Proof Tracker',
                                      'Planned Publish Date' >= Today() &&
                                      'Planned Publish Date' <= DateAdd(Today(), 365, "Days")
                                  ),
                                  EntryDateKey,
                                  Date(Year('Planned Publish Date'), Month('Planned Publish Date'), Day('Planned Publish Date'))
                              )
                          );
                          Set(
                              varDaysToNextThursday,
                              If(Mod(5 - Weekday(Today()) + 7, 7) = 0, 7, Mod(5 - Weekday(Today()) + 7, 7))
                          );
                          ClearCollect(
                              colUpcomingCounts,
                              GroupBy(colUpcomingEntries, EntryDateKey, GroupedEntries)
                          );
                          Clear(colThursdays);
                          ForAll(
                              Sequence(52),
                              If(
                                  (
                                      varSelectedRelease.'High Priority' ||
                                      varDaysToNextThursday + (Value - 1) * 7 >= 10
                                  ) &&
                                  IsBlank(StandDownReason(DateAdd(Today(), varDaysToNextThursday + (Value - 1) * 7, "Days"))) = true,
                                  With(
                                      {thurDate: DateAdd(Today(), varDaysToNextThursday + (Value - 1) * 7, "Days")},
                                      With(
                                          {matchedGroup: LookUp(colUpcomingCounts, EntryDateKey = thurDate)},
                                          With(
                                              {cnt: If(IsBlank(matchedGroup), 0, CountRows(matchedGroup.GroupedEntries))},
                                              Collect(
                                                  colThursdays,
                                                  {
                                                      ThursdayDate: thurDate,
                                                      ThursdayLabel: Text(thurDate, "dddd d mmmm yyyy") &
                                                          If(
                                                              cnt = 0, " - clear",
                                                              cnt = 1, " - 1 entry already scheduled",
                                                              " - " & Text(cnt, "0") & " entries already scheduled"
                                                          ),
                                                      ItemCount: cnt
                                                  }
                                              )
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              )
                          );
                          Set(varSelectedDuplicateDate, First(colThursdays).ThursdayDate);
                          Set(varShowDuplicateModal1, true)

                        PaddingRight: =12
                        PaddingTop: =5

                                                          With(

                                                                  _daysSincePreviousThursday:
                                                                      If(
                                                                          Mod(Weekday(Today()) - 5 + 7, 7) = 0,
                                                                          7,
                                                                          Mod(Weekday(Today()) - 5 + 7, 7)
                                                                      )

                                                              (IsBlank(varTypeFilter) || varTypeFilter = "" || 'Document Type'.Value = varTypeFilter)
                                                          )
                                                      )

10Test

11Save to the Vault

Save App.Formulas, App.OnStart and ViewItem back into the Screen YAML Vault, then tell me, so I can check what is really in the app.

Setting a real stand-down (day to day)
  1. Open the PolicyTracker-StandDownPeriods list and add a row: Title (the reason), StartDate, EndDate, Active Yes.
  2. Every Thursday from StartDate to EndDate, both included, is locked. It takes effect the next time someone opens an item or starts the app. Someone who already has an item open will not see it until they reopen it.
  3. To lift it early, set Active to No or delete the row. Periods whose EndDate has passed are ignored automatically.

Things to know. Items already booked inside a new stand-down are not moved or flagged. If someone opens one, the red note appears and they must pick another Thursday to save. Only dates from today onwards are blocked, so old items in the past are never affected. You can have as many periods as you like. If the list cannot be read, the app shows a warning and carries on without the stand-down.

If Studio shows red after pasting. A red mark on PolicyTracker-StandDownPeriods means the list was not added as a data source. A red mark on Active, Title, StartDate or EndDate means a column name differs from the table in step 1.
